| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:3](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L3) | Vietnamese | `scripts/services/` 是具体能力实现层。 |

| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:10](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L10) | Vietnamese | 统一中间文档结构版本定义、adapter registry、defaults 收口、schema 校验与 normalization report。 |

| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:16](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L16) | Vietnamese | OCR 解析、翻译编排元数据、策略过滤、LLM 调用、结果回填。 |

| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:20](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L20) | Vietnamese | 设计原则： |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:22](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L22) | Vietnamese | - `services/*` 负责把单项能力做完整 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:23](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L23) | Vietnamese | - `ocr_provider/` 只定义 provider 接入约定，不承担具体 provider 实现 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:24](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L24) | Vietnamese | - `document_schema/` 负责定义统一中间层，不承载 provider 细节 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:25](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L25) | Vietnamese | - OCR provider 原始 JSON 必须先经过 `document_schema/adapters.py` 转成 `document.v1` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:26](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L26) | Vietnamese | - 需要排查 raw -> normalized 转化时，优先看 `document.v1.report.json` 或 `validate_document_schema.py --adapt` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:27](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L27) | Vietnamese | - 如果只是消费 provider / defaults / validation 摘要，优先走 `document_schema/reporting.py` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:28](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L28) | Vietnamese | - `mineru/` 是一个 provider 实现，不是 OCR 总工作流本身 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:29](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L29) | Vietnamese | - `pipeline_shared/` 是中性共享层，不应该再放 provider 私有逻辑 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:30](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L30) | Vietnamese | - `translation/ocr` 主线优先读取 normalized document，而不是直接依赖某个 OCR provider 的原始 JSON |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:31](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L31) | Vietnamese | - `runtime/pipeline` 只负责把这些能力串起来 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:32](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L32) | Vietnamese | - 上层入口优先依赖 `runtime/pipeline`，不要直接跨服务拼流程 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:33](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L33) | Vietnamese | - 公共配置和共享工具继续下沉到 `foundation/` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:35](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L35) | Vietnamese | ## 新 OCR Provider 最短路径 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:37](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L37) | Vietnamese | 新 provider 接入时，推荐最短路径是： |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:39](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L39) | Vietnamese | 1. 先读 `ocr_provider/README.md` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:40](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L40) | Vietnamese | 2. 再读 `document_schema/README.md` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:41](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L41) | Vietnamese | 3. 准备最小 raw fixture |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:42](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L42) | Vietnamese | 4. 写 provider API 接入层和 adapter |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:43](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L43) | Vietnamese | 5. 把 fixture 加到 `devtools/tests/document_schema/fixtures/registry.py` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:44](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L44) | Vietnamese | 6. 跑 `devtools/tests/document_schema/regression_check.py` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:46](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L46) | Vietnamese | 只有这条链跑通后，provider 才应该进入 translation/rendering 主线。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:48](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L48) | Vietnamese | ## 协作规矩 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:50](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L50) | Vietnamese | 现在可以按模块拆分负责人，但边界必须按协议来守： |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:52](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L52) | Vietnamese | - OCR / provider 负责人主要维护 `ocr_provider/`、`mineru/`、`document_schema/` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:53](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L53) | Vietnamese | - 翻译负责人主要维护 `translation/` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:54](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L54) | Vietnamese | - 渲染负责人主要维护 `rendering/` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:55](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L55) | Vietnamese | - 编排负责人主要维护 `runtime/pipeline/` |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:57](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L57) | Vietnamese | 默认原则： |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:59](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L59) | Vietnamese | - 每个负责人优先在自己模块内解决问题，不把临时特判扩散到别的模块 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:60](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L60) | Vietnamese | - `document.v1.json`、`translation-manifest.json`、render-only 输入协议属于稳定交接点，不能单边修改 |
| [backend/scripts/services/README.md:61](../../../backend/scripts/services/README.md#L61) | Vietnamese | - 如果必须改交接协议，必须同时更新上下游 README、调用入口、兼容逻辑和测试 |

| [backend/scripts/services/translation/services/policy/README.md:19](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/services/policy/README.md#L19) | Vietnamese | - 策略层只处理 payload 级别判断，不直接碰 PDF 或渲染。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:1](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L1) | Vietnamese | # 翻译 Workflow 边界 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:3](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L3) | Vietnamese | `services.translation.workflow` 是全书翻译的编排层。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:4](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L4) | Vietnamese | 它负责把稳定协议、策略/上下文准备、LLM 执行、结果回填、落盘、诊断和事件串起来， |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:5](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L5) | Vietnamese | 但不应该让某一个文件同时承担所有职责。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:7](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L7) | Vietnamese | ## 当前入口 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:10](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L10) | Vietnamese | 对外请求对象和执行入口。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:12](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L12) | Vietnamese | 根据 OCR JSON、策略配置、术语表、上下文和 provider 诊断配置生成不可变执行计划。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:14](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L14) | Vietnamese | 执行计划，并写出 manifest、review、diagnostics 等汇总产物。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:16](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L16) | Vietnamese | 当前全书翻译阶段顺序。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:18](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L18) | Vietnamese | ## 目标目录 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:21](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L21) | Vietnamese | 后续承接目前集中在 `stages.py` 里的阶段实现。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:22](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L22) | Vietnamese | 一个 phase 可以调用 policy、continuation、LLM 或 repair 服务，但事件格式和落盘细节要收窄。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:25](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L25) | Vietnamese | 后续承接队列分配、批次 worker、结果 drain、tail retry 和 flush 策略。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:26](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L26) | Vietnamese | 这些逻辑目前分散在 `batch_runner.py`、`workers.py` 和 `batching/pending_units.py`。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:29](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L29) | Vietnamese | 后续承接旧的逐页翻译兼容路径。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:30](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L30) | Vietnamese | 当前主要是 `translation_workflow.py`，以及仍然需要它的 debug-only 调用方。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:33](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L33) | Vietnamese | 批次构造规则：去重、低风险可合批判断、fast-path 规划和 pending-unit 选择。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:34](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L34) | Vietnamese | 它不应该负责 provider transport，也不应该负责页面文件落盘。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:36](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L36) | Vietnamese | ## 边界规则 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:38](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L38) | Vietnamese | - Workflow 可以编排 services，但不应该包含 provider 专属 HTTP 逻辑。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:39](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L39) | Vietnamese | - Workflow 可以发送 pipeline events，但事件契约必须稳定，不能靠 log message 推断阶段。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:40](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L40) | Vietnamese | - Batch scheduling 不应该决定翻译质量策略，只负责执行已准备好的 units，并暴露结构化失败。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:41](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L41) | Vietnamese | - Result flush 不应该重建全局 translation-unit 状态，除非调用方明确要求。 |
| [backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md:42](../../../backend/scripts/services/translation/workflow/README.md#L42) | Vietnamese | - Rendering prewarm 属于 runtime/pipeline 职责，translation 内部不应 import rendering 模块。 |
